सं. 67] नई दिल्‍ली, शुक्रवार, अक्तूबर 9, 2026/आश्विन 17, 1948 
No. 67] NEW DELHI, FRIDAY, OCTOBER 9, 2026/ASVINA 17, 1948

अधिसूचना 
नई दिल्‍ली, 9 अक्तूबर, 2026 
का.नि.आ. 67(3).--अहमदाबाद छावनी भवन उप-विधियां, 2026 का निम्नलिखित प्रारूप, जिसे छावनी 
बोर्ड, छावनी अधिनियम, 2006 की धारा 251 द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए और बॉम्बे सरकार 
का राजपत्र, भाग | दिनांक 10 जून, 1926 में प्रकाशित अधिसूचना संख्या 5406 जी/24 दिनांक 1 मार्च, 
1926 को अधिक्रमित करते हुए, सिवाय उन कार्यों के जो ऐसे अधिक्रमण से पहले किए गए या नहीं किए 
गए, जारी करने का प्रस्ताव करता है; अहमदाबाद छावनी भवन उप-विधियां, 2026 का यह प्रारूप, इससे 
प्रभावित होने की संभावना वाली जनता की जानकारी के लिए एतहूवारा प्रकाशित किया जाता है; साथ ही, 
यह सूचना दी जाती है कि उक्त प्रारूप उप-विधियों पर उस तारीख से तीस दिनों की अवधि समाप्त होने के 
बाद पर या उसके पश्चात्‌ विचार किया जाएगा, जिस तारीख को इन उप-विधियों को अंतर्विष्ट करने वाली भारत 
के राजपत्र की प्रतियां जनता के लिए उपलब्ध कराई जाती हैं; और उक्त प्रारूप उप-विधियां, छावनी बोर्ड, 
अहमदाबाद के कार्यालय में और वेबसाइट ahmedabad.cantt.gov.in पर भी उपलब्ध हैं। 
कोई भी व्यक्ति जो प्रारूप उप-विधियों में अंतर्विष्ट प्रस्तावों पर कोई आपत्ति या सुझाव देना चाहता 
है, वह छावनी बोर्ड के विचारार्थ, विनिर्दिष्ट अवधि के भीतर, अहमदाबाद छावनी बोर्ड, को या ईमेल पते : 
ceoahmeda-stats@nic.in पर लिखित रूप में भेज सकता है।

अनुसार भारतीय वास्तुकला परिषद (काउंसिल ऑफ आर्किटेक्चर, इंडिया) के पास वैध

से हवादार क्षेत्र जिसमें कोई मध्यवर्ती फर्श नहीं होता है और जिसका उपयोग आवागमन 
स्थान या प्रवेश फ़ोयर के रूप में किया जाता है; 

‘Samar का अर्थ एक क्षैतिज प्रक्षेप (horizontal projection) जिसमें रेलींग (हैंडरेल),

रहने के साथ एक मार्ग या बैठने के स्थान के रूप में कार्य करता है, सिवाय सुरक्षा के 
लिए रेलिंग या पैरापेट दीवार के; 

“बाधा मुक्त” का अर्थ एक बाधा मुक्त वातावरण जो विकलांग लोगों को सुरक्षित और 
स्वतंत्र रूप से चलने और निर्मित वातावरण के भीतर सुविधाओं का उपयोग करने में 
सक्षम बनाता है; 

“बरसाती” का अर्थ एक शेड या ढका हुआ स्थान, जो सभी तरफ से बंद नहीं होता है, 
जिसका उपयोग छत या भवन पर आश्रय के लिए किया जाता है; 


“बेसमेंट” का अर्थ जमीनी स्तर या आस-पास के जमीनी स्तर के नीचे या आंशिक रूप 
से नीचे भवन की सबसे निचली मंजिल; 
‘ae का अर्थ छावनी अधिनियम, 2006 के तहत गठित एक छावनी बोर्ड;

वाली संरचना चाहे वह चिनाई, ईंट, लकड़ी, मिट्टी, धातु या अन्य सामग्री की हो और 
उसका कोई भी हिस्सा और इसमें एक कुआं और एक दीवार शामिल है जो कि एक 
सीमा दीवार के अलावा हो लेकिन इसमें तंबू या अन्य पोर्टेबल और अस्थायी आश्रय 
शामिल नहीं है;

3. भवन स्वीकृति आवश्यकता- (1) कोई भी व्यक्ति छावनी अधिनियम 2006 के प्रावधानों 
के अनुसार, यथास्थिति, बोर्ड या मुख्य कार्यकारी अधिकारी से ऐसे भवन के लिए पहले 
एक अलग भवन स्वीकृति प्राप्त किए बिना और उसके लिए मुख्य कार्यकारी अधिकारी 
से कार्य प्रारंभ प्रमाण पत्र प्राप्त किए बिना किसी भी भवन का कोई निर्माण, पुनः निर्माण 
नहीं करेगा, उसमें अतिरिक्त निर्माण या परिवर्तन नहीं करेगा या ऐसा नहीं करवाएगा; 
(2) बर्शरते कि, यथास्थिति, बोर्ड या मुख्य कार्यकारी अधिकारी द्वारा जारी की गई कोई भी 
स्वीकृति, जो अधिनियम की धारा 243 के तहत समाप्त हो गई है, इन उप-विधियों के

प्रदान की गई अवधि के लिए मान्य होगी।

a) मेजेनाइन फर्श (मध्यवर्ती तल) में, किसी भी उद्देश्य के लिए बंद छत और Oe

जाएगा, लेकिन यदि मेजेनाइन फर्श (मध्यवर्ती तल) आंतरिक वास्तुशिल्प सजावटी 
उद्देश्य और पर्याप्त हैंडरेल के साथ अन्य मार्ग उद्देश्यों के लिए निर्मित किया गया 
है, तो उसे FSI में नहीं गिना जाएगा। 

b) यदि मेजेनाइन फर्श (मध्यवर्ती तल) का आकार 9.5 वर्ग मीटर या उससे अधिक 
है, तो जहां तक प्रकाश और वेंटिलेशन का संबंध है, इसे लिविंग रूम के मानक 
के अनुरूप होना चाहिए। 

०) इसका निर्माण इस प्रकार किया गया है कि किसी भी परिस्थिति में इसके ऊपर 
और नीचे के स्थान के वेंटिलेशन में बाधा न आए; 

d) ऐसा मेजेनाइन फर्श या उसका कोई हिस्सा We के रूप में उपयोग नहीं 
किया जाएगा। 

०) किसी भी मामले में मेजेनाइन फर्श (मध्यवर्ती तल) को बंद नहीं किया जाएगा

हो।

26. गैराज (GARAGE)-(1) व्यक्तिगत निजी गैराज: आवासीय भवन में निजी गैराज का 
आकार 2.5 मीटर x 5.0 मीटर से कम नहीं होगा। यदि गैराज OY के खुले स्थान में 
स्थित है, तो इसका निर्माण मुख्य भवन से 1.5 मीटर के भीतर नहीं किया जाएगा। 


(2) व्यावसायिक / सार्वजनिक गैराज: सार्वजनिक गैराज का आकार पार्क किए जाने वाले 
वाहनों की संख्या और प्रत्येक वाहन के लिए नीचे निर्दिष्ट न्यूनतम पार्किंग स्थान के आधार 
पर किया जाएगा:

की पार्किंग शामिल है॥

बेसमेंट में निम्नलिखित आवश्यकताएं होंगी, अर्थात: - 


प्रत्येक बेसमेंट फर्श से is या छत के नीचे के हिस्से तक प्रत्येक भाग में कम

बेसमेंट के लिए पर्याप्त वेंटिलेशन प्रदान किया जाएगा। वेंटिलेशन के मानक वही 
होंगे जो उप-विधियों के अनुसार विशेष अधिभोग के लिए आवश्यक हैं। किसी 
भी कमी को 50 वर्ग मीटर फर्श क्षेत्र के लिए एक निकास पंखे की दर से 
ब्लोअर या निकास tal (exhaust fans) & रूप में पर्याप्त यांत्रिक वेंटिलेशन 
प्रदान करके पूरा किया जा सकता है। 

किसी भी बेसमेंट की छत की न्यूनतम ऊंचाई औसत आसपास के जमीनी स्तर 
से 0.9 मीटर ऊपर और अधिकतम 1.2 मीटर होगी। 

पर्याप्त व्यवस्था की जाएगी ताकि सतह की जल निकासी या जल निकासी का 
पानी बेसमेंट में प्रवेश न करे। बेसमेंट की दीवारें और फर्श जल-रोधी होंगे। 
बेसमेंट को विभाजित नहीं किया जाएगा। जिन मामलों में सक्षम प्राधिकारी द्वारा 
विभाजनों की अनुमति दी जाती है और ऊपर (७) में निर्धारित वेंटिलेशन मानकों 
को पूरा किया जाता है, वहां बेसमेंट के विभाजन को इस शर्त के अधीन स्वीकृत 
किया जा सकता है कि कोई भी स्थान 50 वर्ग मीटर से कम न हो। 


आग के खिलाफ पर्याप्त सुरक्षा प्रदान की जाएगी। बेसमेंट और उसके ऊपर के 
फर्शों को अलग करने वाली छतों का निर्माण आर.सी.सी. जैसी सामग्री या ऐसी 
सामग्री से किया जाएगा जो कम से कम दो घंटे तक आग के प्रति प्रतिरोध प्रदान 
करेगी। 

जहां अपार्टमेंट घरों (आवासीय फ्लैटों) और होटलों में बेसमेंट स्वीकृत किया जाता

की छत में थिम्बल्स प्रदान किए जाएंगे और योजना पर उनकी स्थिति स्पष्ट रूप

बेसमेंट की दीवारें और फर्श जल-रोधी होंगे और इस प्रकार डिजाइन किए जाएंगे 
कि आसपास की मिट्टी की नमी के प्रभाव, यदि कोई हो, को ध्यान में रखा जाए 
और पर्याप्त डैम्प प्रूफिंग उपचार दिया जाए। 

वायुमंडलीय पानी के बेसमेंट में रिसने की स्थिति में उसे बाहर निकालने के लिए 
आवश्यक व्यवस्था प्रदान की जाएगी। 

बेसमेंट में रसोई, SAR और वाटर aie की स्वीकृति तब तक नहीं दी 
जाएगी जब तक कि सीवर का स्तर इसकी अनुमति न देता हो और सीवेज के 
बैकफ्लो और बाढ़ की कोई संभावना न हो। यदि स्वीकृत किया जाता है तो उन्हें 
बेसमेंट की बाहरी दीवारों (जो भवन की बाहरी दीवारें भी होंगी) के पास रखा 
जाएगा और वे पर्याप्त रूप से प्रकाशित और हवादार होंगे। ऐसे रसोईघरों और 
GAR! का क्षेत्रफल FSI में गिना जाएगा। 

बेसमेंट में स्वीकृत होने पर रसोईघर केवल इलेक्ट्रिक ओवन, स्टोव, गैस से 
सुसज्जित होगा। किसी कोक या जलाऊ लकड़ी का उपयोग नहीं किया जाएगा। 
बेसमेंट तक पहुंच मुख्य और वैकल्पिक सीढ़ी से अलग होगी जो ऊपरी मंजिलों 
से पहुंच और निकास प्रदान करती है। जहां सीढ़ी निरंतर है, वह बंद प्रकार की 
होगी जो बेसमेंट फर्श और ऊपरी मंजिलों से अग्नि अलगाव (॥#8 separation) 
के रूप में कार्य करेगी। खुले रैंप को स्वीकृत किया जाएगा यदि वे उपयुक्त जल 
निकासी प्रावधान के अधीन भवन रेखा के भीतर निर्मित किए गए हों। 

कार्यालय और वाणिज्यिक अधिभोग के बेसमेंट के मामले में, समय-समय पर

संख्या में निकास मार्ग और पहुंच मार्ग प्रदान किए जाएंगे।

चिमनियाँ छत के शीर्ष से कम से कम 0.9 मीटर ऊपर बनाई जाएँगी, बशर्ते कि शीर्ष 
चिमनी आसन्न पैरापेट दीवार के शीर्ष से नीचे न हो। ढालू छत के मामले में, चिमनी का 
शीर्ष उस छत के Re (ridge) से कम से कम 0.6 मीटर ऊपर होगा जिसमें चिमनी 
प्रवेश करती है। 

कमरों का प्रकाश और वेंटिलेशन-(1) मानव निवास के लिए अभिप्रेत एक या अधिक 
कमरों के एक हिस्से का पूरा या कुछ हिस्सा जो सामने, पीछे या UY के खुले स्थानों 
पर नहीं सटता है, वह एक आंतरिक खुले स्थान पर सटेगा जिसकी सभी दिशाओं में 
न्यूनतम चौड़ाई 12.5 मीटर से अधिक ऊंचाई न होने वाले भवनों के मामले में 3.0 
मीटर होगी और 12.5 मीटर से अधिक प्रत्येक मीटर ऊंचाई या उसके हिस्से के लिए 
0.3 मीटर की बढ़ती ऊंचाई के साथ इसे बढ़ाने के प्रावधान के अधीन होगी। हालांकि,

अध्याय VI 

बचत, दंड और निर्वचन | 
बचत खंडः-(1) बोर्ड द्वारा स्वीकृत मौजूदा भवन मान्य रहेंगे और उप-विधियों के लागू 
होने की तारीख से पहले बोर्ड द्वारा स्वीकृत भवन योजनाओं के अनुसार निर्मित भवन 
भी मान्य होंगे। 
आवासीय भवन के नए निर्माण के लिए प्लॉट क्षेत्र के 50 वर्ग मीटर के न्यूनतम आकार 
का प्रतिबंध रहेगा। हालांकि, यह प्रतिबंध 50 वर्ग मीटर से कम के प्लॉटों पर भवनों के 
पुनः निर्माण के लिए लागू नहीं होगा जहां भवन एक मौजूदा अधिकृत संरचना है और 
निर्माण का प्लॉट आकार सामान्य भूमि रजिस्टर (GLR) F क्षेत्र के अनुसार है। 
जहां कहीं भी कोई मौजूदा स्वीकृत संरचना/भवन है जो कवरेज और एफएआर/एफएसआई 
(FAR/FSI) आदि से अधिक है, वहां इन उप-विधियों के कार्यान्वयन से पहले मूल 
संरचना को दी गई स्वीकृति के अनुसार पुनः निर्माण की अनुमति दी जाएगी। 
दंड. -जो कोई भी इन उप-विधियों के किसी भी प्रावधान का उल्लंघन करेगा, वह 
अधिनियम के प्रावधानों के अनुसार दंडनीय होगा। 
निर्ववन अधिनियम, लागू नियमों और नीतियों के अनुरूप होगा:- सके अंतर्गत किसी 
भी बात के होते हुए भी, भवन उप-विधियों के प्रावधान हर समय अधिनियम के प्रावधानों 
और अधिनियम के तहत अन्य वैधानिक नियमों के अनुरूप और उनके भीतर होंगे। जहां 
कहीं भी कोई विसंगति या अस्पष्टता उत्पन्न होती है, वहां अधिनियम लागू होगा और वही 
मान्य होगा। अधिनियम, 2006 के प्रावधान इन उप-विधियों में अंतर्निहित हैं और उप- 
विधियों को समझने और लागू करने के लिए उप-विधियों के भाग के रूप में पढ़े जाएंगे। 
इसी तरह, US की शर्तें, पुराने अनुदान की शर्तें और मौजूदा नीतियों या इस संबंध में 
समय-समय पर जारी किसी भी नीति के अनुसार ऐसी अन्य शर्तें लागू होंगी और अब 
Renta भवन उप-विधियां इन नीतियों के अर्थ के भीतर होंगे और उक्त नीति की शर्तों 
के किसी भी तरह से विपरीत नहीं होंगे। oad सीमा तक, इन भवन उप-विधियों की 
शर्तें सशर्त हैं।

दौरान इन प्रावधानों का पालन किया जाएगा।

गया है। कार्य हमारी संतुष्टि के अनुसार पूरा किया गया है और उपयोग की गई कारीगरी 
और सामग्रियां सामान्य और विस्तृत विनिर्देशों का पूरी तरह से पालन करती हैं। जल 
निकासी/स्वच्छता /जलापूर्ति के कार्य भवन उप-विधियों और स्वीकृत योजना के अनुसार 
निष्पादित किए गए हैं। कार्य के दौरान भवन उप-विधियों और निर्धारित शर्तों, या उसके 
तहत जारी आदेशों के किसी भी प्रावधान का उल्लंघन नहीं किया गया है। भवन उस 
उपयोग के लिए उपयुक्त है जिसके लिए इसका निर्माण/पुनः निर्माण या परिवर्तन/निर्माण 
और विस्तार किया गया है। 

2). प्रमाण पत्र:- 


2.1). प्रमाणित किया जाता है कि भवन/भवनों का निर्माण स्वीकृत योजना और संरचनात्मक 
डिजाइन के अनुसार किया गया है जिसमें प्रासंगिक प्रचलित आईएस 
कोड/मानक/दिशानिर्देशों में निर्दिष्ट संरचनात्मक सुरक्षा के प्रावधान शामिल हैं। 


2.2). प्रमाणित किया जाता है कि स्वीकृत भवन योजना के अनुसार जल संचयन के साथ-साथ 
अपशिष्ट जल पुनर्चक्रण प्रणाली प्रदान की गई है।

का पालन करता है।

MINISTRY OF DEFENCE 
NOTIFICATION 
New Delhi, the 9th October, 2026 


S.R.O. 67(E) .—The following draft of the Anmedabad Cantonment Building 
Bye-Laws, 2026, which the Cantonment Board proposes to issue in exercise of the 
powers conferred by Section 251 of the Cantonments Act, 2006 and in supersession 
of notification Number 5406 G/24 dated 1S March, 1926 published in Bombay 
Government Gazette, Part | dated 107 June, 1926, except as respects things done or 
omitted to be done before such supersession, is hereby published for the information 
of the public likely to be affected thereby; and notice is hereby given that the said draft 
Bye-laws shall be taken into consideration on or after the expiry of a period of thirty 
days from the date on which copies of the Gazette of India containing the Bye-laws 
are made available to the Public; and the said draft Bye-Laws is also available at the 
office of the Cantonment Board, Anmedabad on the website ahmedabad.cantt.gov.in 


Any person interested in making any objections or suggestions on the 
proposals contained in the draft Bye-Laws may forward the same in writing, for 
consideration of the Cantonment Board within the period so specified to the 
Cantonment Board, Anmedabad or at email address: ceoahmeda-stats@nic.in 


(wT Wave 4] भारत का राजपत्र : असाधारण 87 


THE DRAFT OF THE AHMEDABAD CANTONMENT BUILDING BYE-LAWS, 2026 
CHAPTER | 


GENERAL 


1. SHORT TITLE, COMMENCEMENT AND EXTENT.~{1) These Bye-Laws 
shall be called the Anmedabad Cantonment Building Bye-laws, 2026. 


(2) | These shall come into force on the date of final publication in the Official 
Gazette by the Central Government. 


(3) | These Bye-Laws shall apply within the limits of Anmedabad Cantonment. 


2. DEFINITIONS.— In these bye-laws, unless the context otherwise requires: 
(1) “Act” means the Cantonments Act, 2006 as amended from time to time; 

(2) “Addition to a building” means addition to the cubic contents of a building; 
(3) “Alteration of building” means- 


(a) The sub-division of any room in such building so as to convert the same 
into two or more separate rooms, or 


(b) The change in two or more rooms in such building which increases or 
diminishes the cubic content of any room or rooms. 


(c) The conversion of any passage or space in such building into a room or 
rooms; 


(4) "Applicant" means a person who has legally valid occupancy rights on leased 


or old grant or private land and intends to erect/re-erect a building or to make 
any addition/alteration in the building; 


(5) “Application” means an application made in such a form as may be specified 
by the authority from time to time; 

(6) "Approved" means approval granted by the Competent Authority as 
prescribed under the Act; 


(7) "Architect" means any person who holds a valid registration with the Council 
of Architecture, India as per the provisions of the Architects Act, 1972; 


(8) “Atrium” means a sky lighted and naturally/mechanically ventilated area in a 
building with no intermediate floors and used as circulation space or entrance 
foyer; 


88 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART II—SEc.4] 


(9) “Balcony” means a horizontal projection including a handrail, balustrade or a 
parapet to serve as a passage or sitting out place with at least one side open, 
except for the railing or parapet wall for safety; 


(10) “Barrier Free” means a barrier free environment which enables people with 
disabilities to move safely and freely and to use the facilities within the built 
environment; 


(11) “Barsati” means a shed or covered space, which is not enclosed on all sides, 
used for shelter on the roof or a building; 


(12) “Basement” means the lowest storey of a building below or partly below 
ground level or adjoining ground level; 


(13) “Board” shall mean a Cantonment Board constituted under the Cantonments 
Act, 2006; 


(14) “Building” means a house, outhouse, stable, latrine, shed, hut or other roofed 
structure whether of masonry, brick, wood, mud, metal or other material and 
any part thereof and includes a well and a wall other than a boundary wall but 
does not include a tent or other portable and temporary shelter; 


(15) “Building Envelope” means the horizontal spatial limits up to which a building 
may be permitted to be constructed on a plot which shall not exceed the clear 
setbacks as prescribed in the Bye-Laws; 


(16) “Building Height” means the vertical distance measured in the case of flat roof 
from the average level of the centre line of the adjoining street to the highest 
points of the building adjacent to the street wall; in the case of pitched roofs up 
to the point where the external surface of the outer wall intersects the finished 
surface of the sloping roof; and in the case of gable facing the road, the midpoint 
between the eaves level and the ridge. Architectural features, chimneys, water 
tanks and other ornamental features which are not intended for human 
habitation and serving no other function except that of decoration shall be 
excluded for the purpose of taking heights, and if the building does not abut on 
a street the height shall be measured above the average level of the ground 
around and contiguous to the building; 


(17) “Building Line” means the line up to which the plinth of a building adjoining on 
a street or on an extension of a street or on a future street may lawfully extend 
and includes the line prescribed, if any, in any scheme; 


(18) “Built-up Area” means the area covered by a building on all floors including 
cantilevered portion, mezzanine floors, if any, but excluding the areas 
specifically exempted from computation of Floor Space Index (F.S.I.) under 
these Regulations; 


fart ave 44 भारत का राजपत्र : असाधारण 89 


(19) “Bungalow Area” means area outside notified civil area with old grant / 
leasehold bungalows/ Private properties; 


(20) “Butchery” means a slaughterhouse where animals (sheep and goats) are 
slaughtered under the supervision of Competent Authority; 


(21) “Cabin” means a non-residential enclosure constructed of non-load bearing, 
non-masonry partitions; 


(22) “Canopy” means an overhead roof or else a structure over which a fabric or 
metal covering is attached, able to provide shade or shelter from weather 
conditions such as sun, hail, snow and rain. A canopy can also be a tent, 
generally without a floor; 


(23) “Ceiling Height” means vertical distance between the finished floor and the 
ceiling; 


(24) “Central Government” for the purposes of these Bye-Laws means the 
Government of the Union in the Ministry of Defence; 


(25) “Chajja” means a sloping or horizontal structural overhang usually provided 
over openings on external walls to provide protection from sun and rain and for 
purpose of architectural appearance; 


26) “Chimney” means an upright shaft containing and encasing one or more flues; 
y 


(27) “Civil Engineer” means any person who is a graduate of civil engineering from 
a recognized Indian or foreign university duly approved by Govt. of India or is a 
member of Civil Engineering Division of the Institution of Engineers (India) and 
is competent to carry out supervision of building works as per approved 
drawings and design; 


(28) “Combustible Material” means any material if it burns or adds heat to a fire 
when tested for combustibility in accordance with good practice; 


(29) “Commercial Building” means a building used or constructed or adapted to 
be used wholly or a substantial part of building for commercial purposes; 


(30) “Competent Authority” means the authority to sanction the building plan as 
prescribed under Chapter 10 of the Cantonments Act, 2006; 


(31) “Corner Plot or Site” means a plot or site at the junction of and fronting on two 
or more intersecting streets; 


(32) “Courtyard” means a space open to the sky enclosed or partially enclosed by 
walls or by railing and shall be at ground floor level; 


(33) “Covered Area” means the ground area covered by the building immediately 
above the plinth level, but does not include the space covered by the following: 


90 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART II—SEc.4] 


(a) Garden, rockery, well and well structure, plant nursery, water pool, 
swimming pool (if uncovered), platform around a tree, water tank, fountain, 
bench with open to pandunen closed sides and like; 


(6) Drainage, culvert, conduit, catch pit, gully pit, chamber, gutter, septic tank, 
soakage pit and the like; 


(c) Compound wall, gate, canopy, uncovered staircases areas covered by 
sunshade and the like. 


(34) “Cupboard” means a space used for storage of household goods/ dress 
having shelves/partitions not more than 2.5 m apart; 


(35) “Damp Proof Course” means a course consisting of appropriate 
waterproofing material provided to prevent penetration of dampness or 
moisture from any part of the ground to the structure or one part of the structure 
to any other part of it; 


(36) “Demolished” means the total dismantling of an existing building; 


(37) “Drain” means a line of pipes including all fittings and equipment such as 
manholes, inspection chambers, traps, gully traps and floor traps, used for the 
drainage of a building, or a number of buildings, or yards appurtenant to 
buildings, within the same curtilage. Drain shall also include open channels 
used for conveying surface water; 


(38) “Drainage” means the removal of any liquid by a system constructed for this 
purpose; 


(39) “Dwelling Unit/ Tenement” means an independent housing unit with separate 
facilities for living, cooking and sanitation needs; 


(40) "Enclosed Staircase" means a staircase separated by fire resistant walls and 
doors from the rest of the buildings; 


(41) “Equivalent Car Space (ECS)” means the space occupied by the vehicle as 
well as the minimum space needed to move it into and out of the space and 
One equivalent car space is as defined in National Building Code of India as 
amended from time to time; 


(42) “Existing Building or Use” means a building, structure or its use as 
sanctioned or deemed to have been sanctioned by the Competent Authority, 
existing before the commencement of the Bye-Laws; 


(43) “Exit” means a passage or means of egress from any buildings, storey or floor 
area to a street or any other open space of safety; 


(a) Vertical exit: It is a means of exit used for ascending or descending 
between two or more levels, including stairways, ramps and fire 
escapes;

(0) Horizontal exit: It is a protected opening through or around a fire- 
resistant wall or bridge connecting two buildings; 


(0) Outside exit: It is an exit from the building to a public way or to an open 
area leading to a public way or an enclosed fire-resistant passage 
leading to a public way; 


“External Wall” means an outer wall of a building not being a partition wall, 
even though adjoining to a wall of another building and also means, a wall 
abutting on an interior open space of any building; 


“Floor” means the lower surface in a storey on which one normally walks in a 
building. The general term, floor, unless otherwise specifically mentioned, shall 
not refer to a mezzanine floor. The sequential numbering of the floor shall be 
determined by its relation to the determining entrance level. For floors at or 
wholly above ground level, the lowest floor in the building with a direct entrance 
from the road/street is to be termed as the Ground Floor. Other floors above 
the ground floor shall be numbered in sequence as Floor 1, Floor 2, with 
numbers increasing upwards; 


“Floor Area Ratio” or “FAR” means the quotient obtained by dividing the 
multiple of the total of the covered area on all floors and hundred by the area of 
the plot, i.e. 


Total covered area on all floors x 100 
FAR = 


Plot Area 


“Floor Space Index” or “FSI” means the quotient obtained by dividing the total 
of the covered area on all floors by the area of the plot, i.e. 


Total covered area on all floors 
FSI = 


Plot Area 


“Flue” means a confined space provided for the conveyance to the outer air of 
any product of combustion resulting from the operation of any heat producing 
appliance or equipment employing solid, liquid or gaseous fuel; 


“Footing” means off set portions at the base of a foundation to provide a 
greater bearing area; 


“Foundation” means that part of the structure which is below the lowermost 
floor and which provides support for the superstructure, and which transmits 
loads of the superstructure to the ground; 


“Front Open Space / Margin / Setback” means the distance between the 
boundary line of plot abutting the means of access / road / street and the 


92 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART II—SEc.4] 


building line. In case of plots facing two or more means of access, roads / 
streets, the plot shall be deemed to front on all such means of accesses / roads 
/ streets; 


(52) “Frontage” means the measurement of the side of any site abutting the road; 


(53) "Gallery" means an intermediate floor or platform projecting from a wall of an 
auditorium or hall providing extra floor, additional seating accommodation etc; 


(54) “Garage Private” means a building design or used for the parking of a motor 
car or other vehicles; 


(55) “Ground Coverage” means the quotient obtained in terms of percentage by 
dividing the plinth areas of the Ground Floor by plot area, i.e. 


Plinth area of ground floor x 100 


Ground Coverage = 
Plot Area 


(56) “Ground Level” means the finishing surface after formation cutting of the site 
from where the erection of the building starts; 


(57) “Group Housing” means housing of more than one dwelling unit, where land 
is owned jointly as in the case of cooperative societies of public agencies such 
as Local Authorities or Housing Boards or property developers etc. and the 
construction is undertaken by one agency but shall not include properties which 
are co- owned by family members; 


(58) “Habitable Room” means a room occupied or designed for occupancy by one 
or more persons for study, living, sleeping, eating, kitchen if it is part of living 
room but does not include bathroom, water closet compartments, laundries, 
serving and storage pantries, corridors, cellars, attics and other spaces that are 
not used frequently or during extended period; 


(59) “Height of a Room” means the vertical distance measured from the finished 
floor surface to the finished ceiling/slab surface; 


(60) “Internal Wall” means all partition walls in the interior of a building excluding 
walls which abut exterior or interior open spaces; internal walls when made of 
bricks shall be a minimum of 115 mm. brickwork in suitable mortar; other non- 
load-bearing partitions shall be of suitable thickness; 


(61) “Loft” means an intermediate floor in between two main floors but not more 
than 1.5 min height which may be adopted or constructed for storage purposes;

(62) “Marginal Distance / Side and Rear Marginal Open Space” means a 
minimum distance required to be left open to sky between the boundary of the 
building plot and the building line on respective sides; 


(63) “Masonry” means the form of construction composed of brick, stone, tile, 
concrete blocks, gypsum or other similar building material units or combination 
of these material units laid up, bonded together and set in mortar; 


(64) “Materials Alteration” means a change of use in building materials in any 
existing building; 


(65) “Mezzanine Floor” means an intermediate floor between two floor levels 
above the ground floor, and at least one side of it should form an integral part 
of the space /floor below; 


(66) “Mumty” means a structure with a covering roof over a staircase and its landing 
built to enclose only the stairs for the purpose of providing protection from 
weather and not meant for human habitation; 


(67) “Non-Combustible” means a material which does not burn or add heat to a 
fire when tested for combustibility in accordance with good practice; 


(68) “Notified Civil Area (NCA)” means an Area declared to be Civil area by the 
Central Government under Sub-section (1) of Section 46 of the Cantonments 
Act, 2006; 


(69) “Occupancy” means the function or use of the building; 


(70) “Parapet” means a low wall built along the edge of a roof or a floor not more 
than 0.9m height; 


(71) “Parking Space” means an area enclosed or unenclosed sufficient in size to 
park vehicles together with a driveway connecting the parking space with a 
street or alley and provided for ingress and egress of the vehicles; 


(72) “Partition” means an interior non-load-bearing wall, one storey or part storey 
in height; 


(73) “Pavilion” means a flexible architectural open space that invites people to 
come in and spend time in it. It could be temporary or permanent and might 
even change its form and function. A pavilion might be used as a shelter, 
seating, meeting point, cafe, theatre, or for lectures, events, exhibitions, sports, 
play, relaxation and work, etc.; 


(74) “Pillar” means a wood, stone, brick, RCC or metal pillar and includes all 
columns or upright posts or support, stanchions (steel structures) and an 
assemblage of columns of stanchions properly riveted or welded or bolted 
together; 


94 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART II—SEc.4] 


(75) “Plinth” means the portion of a structure between the surface of the 
surrounding ground and the surface of the floor immediately above the ground; 


(76) “Porch or Portico” means a covered surface supported on pillars or otherwise 
for the purpose of pedestrian or vehicular approach to an entrance in a building; 


(77) “Public Building” means a building used or intended to be used either 
ordinarily or occasionally, as a church, temple, mosque or any other place of 
public worship, dharmshala, college/school, hostel, theatre, cinema, public 
concert room, lecture room, library, orphanage, or rescue home or any other 
place of public assembly; 


(78) “Public Garage” means a building or portion thereof designed other than a 
private garage, operated for gains, designed or used for repairing, servicing, 
hiring, selling or storing motor driven or other vehicles;

“Residential Building” means a building used or constructed or adapted to be 
used wholly for human habitation; 


“Residential cum Commercial Building” means a building where part or the 
building is used or constructed or intended to be used for human habitation with 
remaining part used or constructed or intended to be used for commercial 
purpose; 


“Sanctioned Plan” means the set of plans and specifications submitted under 
section 235 of the Cantonments Act, 2006, under the Bye-Laws and regulations 
in connection with a building and duly sanctioned by the Authority under Section 
234/237/238 of the Cantonments Act, 2006; 


“Set Back Line” means a line usually parallel with the center line of the road 
or street and laid down in each case by the Board beyond which nothing can 
be constructed towards the road and street; 


“Site or plot” means a parcel or piece of land enclosed by definite boundaries; 


“Smoke Pipe” means a flue approximately horizontal, made of metal or other 
material in which smoke or the products of combustion are conducted from a 
furnace to a chimney; 


“Store or Shop” shall mean any store or shop in which it is not intended that 
any person shall reside; 


“Storey” means the portion of a building included between the surface of any 
floor and the surface of the floor next above it, or if there be no floor above it, 
then the space between any floor and the ceiling next above it; 


"Street" includes any way, road, lane, square, court, alley or passage in a 
Cantonment, whether a thoroughfare or not and whether built upon or not, over 
which the public have a right of way and also the road way on foot-way over 
any bridge or cause way; 


“Structural Engineer” means a person who is a graduate of civil engineering 
from a recognized Indian or Foreign university duly approved by the 
Government of India or is a member of the Civil Engineering Division of the 
Institution of Engineers (India) with experience in structural engineering 
practice, which includes structural design and field work; 


“Sun Shade” means a sloping or horizontal structural overhang usually 
provided over openings on an external wall to provide protection from sun and 
rain; 


96 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART II—SEc.4] 


(91) “Swimming Pool” means a wading pool, paddling pool or simply a pool in a 
container filled with water intended for swimming or water-based recreation. 
The pool can be built either above or in the ground and from materials such as 
concrete, metal, plastic or fiber glass; 


(92) “To Abut” means to be positioned juxtaposed to a road, lane, open space, 
building etc; 
(93) “To erect or Re-erect” means 
(a) To erect anew building on any site, whether previously built upon or not; 


(6) To re-erect any building of which portion have been pulled down, burnt 
or destroyed, damaged by storm, rains, fire, earthquake or any other 
natural calamity to the same extent, and similar specifications as existing 
prior to damage; 


(c) Conversion from one occupancy to another; 
(d) Tocarry out alterations and; 
(e) To add to a storey / storeys; 


(94) “Total Floor Area” means the area of all floors of the building, including 
habitable, basement and mezzanine floor; 


(95) “Travel Distance” means the distance an occupant has to travel to reach the 
exit; 


(96) “Verandah” means a covered area with at least one side open to the outside; 


(97) “Warehouse” means a building, the whole or a substantial part of which is 
used or intended to be used for the storage of goods, whether for keeping or 
for sale or for any similar purpose but does not include a storeroom attached to 
and used for the proper functioning of a shop; 


(98) “Washing Platform” means an authorized platform for washing and also for 
servicing of scooters/cars, etc; 


(99) “Water Closet (WC)” means a privy with arrangement for flushing the pan with 
water but does not include bathroom; 


(100) “Water Course” means a natural channel meant for carrying storm water and 
includes an artificial one formed by training or diversion of a natural channel; 


(a) “Major Water Course” means a river. 
(b) “Minor Water Course” means a nallah; 


(101) “Window” means an opening to the outside other than a door which provides 
all or part of the required natural light, ventilation or both to an interior space 
and not used as a means of ingress/egress; 

(102) “Workshop” means a room or a group of rooms or buildings in which goods 
are manufactured or repaired; 

Words and expressions used and not defined in these Bye-laws but defined in 
the Cantonments Act — 2006 as amended from time to time shall have the same 
meanings as assigned to them in the Act; 


(art Wats 4] भारत का राजपत्र : असाधारण 


CHAPTER II 
PROCEDURE FOR SANCTION OF BUILDING PLANS AND RELATED 
DOCUMENTATION 


3. BUILDING SANCTION REQUIRED.—(1) No person shall carry out any erection, 
re-erection make additions or alterations to any building or cause the same to be 
done without first obtaining a separate building sanction for such building from the 
Board or the Chief Executive Officer as the case may be as per the Provisions of 
Cantonment Act 2006, and without obtaining a commencement certificate from 
the Chief Executive officer therefore; 


(2) Provided that any sanction issued by the Board or the Chief Executive Officer as 
the case may be, which has lapsed under Section 243 of the Act shall not be 
renewed except in accordance with the provisions of these Bye-Laws; 


(3) Notice for erection or Re-erection of building.— 

a) An applicant who intends to erect or re-erect a building or to make addition or 
alteration in any building shall give notice on echhawani portal to the Chief 
Executive Officer, of the said intention in prescribed Form-A as per the 
Appendix; 

b) Such notice shall be accompanied by drawings and documents as per 
procedure prescribed on echhawani.

PROCEDURE FOR OBTAINING BUILDING SANCTION.—{1) An applicant who 
intends to erect or re-erect a building or to make an addition or alteration in any 
building shall register himself/ herself in the e-Chhawani portal and apply online, 
duly enclosing all the requisite documents and plans in the Ahmedabad e- 
Chhawani Portal through the Cantonment Board registered architect/ architects: 


102 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART II—SEc.4] 


Provided that, due to any reasons, if it is not feasible for an applicant to apply in 
the e-Chhawani portal, the Board may prescribe a procedure for the submission 
of applications: 


Provided further that the Central Government or Director General may issue 
guidelines from time to time to the Board regarding the manner of submitting 
applications for the said purpose. 


S 


Only persons who have legally valid occupancy rights on leased or old grant or 
private land are eligible for applying for the sanction of the Building Plan. In cases 
wherein breach of lease conditions on leased land or breach of old grant terms in 
old grant land are involved, the Board shall have the right to refuse or reject 
applications for erections, re-erection or addition/alteration till the breach of lease 
or old grant conditions is regularized or condoned. 


(3) On scrutiny of the documents and plans submitted, the applicant receives the 
Development Charges communication (or the charges levied by the Board by 
whatever name these are known) for payment to the Cantonment Board. 


(4) On the deposit of Development Charges (or the charges levied by the Board by 
whatever name these are known), the sanction is conveyed within the prescribed 
timelines. 


(5) List of documents to be submitted for building permission is covered in Section 7 
“DOCUMENTS AT THE TIME OF APPLICATION”. 


5. GUIDELINES FOR THE QUALIFICATIONS AND COMPETENCE OF 
PROFESSIONALS.—Every building / development work for which permission is 
sought under the code shall be planned, designed and supervised by registered 
professionals. The registered professionals for carrying out the various activities 
shall be: - (a) architect (b) engineer (c) structural engineer (d) supervisor (e) town 
planner, (f) landscape architect (g) urban designer and (h) utility service engineer. 
The requirements of registration and competence of such registered personnel to 
carry out various activities shall be decided by Board/CEO through Cantonment 
Board Resolution and revised from time to time. 


6. BUILDING APPLICATION FEES AND APPLICABLE CHARGES.—{1) Building 
Application Fees: The Building Plan application shall be deemed valid only if the 
applicant has paid the requisite building application fee as determined by the 
Board from time to time. 


Note: Building permit fee shall be fixed and revised uniformly by the Cantonment 
Board / Chief Executive Officer, as the case may be, from time to time. 


(2) Security Deposit Fee.— For ensuring faithful compliance of regulations and the 
directions given in the sanctioned plan and the other terms and conditions a 
Security Fee / sq.m. (as decided by Board) of plot area shall be charged. The

same shall be returned without interest to the owner after issue of full Occupancy

the Cantonment Act 2006 shall be initiated. The Cantonment Board / Chief 
Executive Officer, as the case may be, shall revise the fees uniformly from time to

Material stacking charges. — Material stacking on streets or other public land 
shall not take place without the permission of the Chief Executive Officer/Board as 
the case may be. The Owner / Applicant shall have to pay material stacking 
charges if the materials are stacked on any property under Management of the 
Cantonment Board. The charges shall be decided by Board of the plot area for a 
period of two years and accordingly the same rates shall be charged for 
subsequent year if the extension is allowed. The charges will have to be paid at 
the time of sanctioning of building plan and subsequently at the time of granting 
extension. The Cantonment Board / Chief Executive Officer may uniformly revise 
the charges from time to time. The Chief Executive Officer may authorize any 
officer not below the rank of Sanitary Inspector for contravention under the above 
Bye-Law. 


Tree Planting Charges.—Tree planting charges shall be charged by Cantonment 
Board at the time of sanctioning of Building plan, charges shall be decided by the 
Board from time to time. 


Development Charges.— The Development Charges, labour cess and other 
fees, as determined by the Board, are to be paid by the applicant online before 
issuance of the sanction of the building application by the Competent Authority. 


Incentive in development charges if Rooftop Solar Installation and Rain 
Water Harvesting is proposed and implemented.—For construction of 
residential or commercial or any building in plot less than 500 sq.m., if the applicant 
opts for Rooftop Solar Installation and/or Rain Water Harvesting, he/she shall be 
eligible for refunding of development charges after the completion of said Rooftop 
solar installation and/or Rain Water Harvesting facility and submission of 
photographs to this effect. The quantum of incentive for different categories of 
buildings shall be decided by the Board by resolution.

8. SANCTION / REFUSAL OF PLANS, VALIDITY PERIOD AND REVALIDATION 
PROCEDURE.—(1) In case of old grant land, the proposed building plan shall be 
in conformity with the old grant terms and the land policies of the Ministry of 
Defence. 


(2) In case of leased land, the proposed building plan shall be in conformity with the 
lease hold rights/terms and the land policies of the Ministry of Defence. 

(3) The Competent Authority shall sanction the plan unless such building would 
contravene any of the following, namely: - 


(i) 


(ii) 


(ii) 


That the building or the use of the site for the building or any of the particulars 
comprised in the site plan, ground plan, elevation, section or specification 
contravenes the provisions of the Act or the Bye-Laws made thereunder; 


That the notice for sanction does not contain the particulars or is not prepared 
in the manner required under the provisions of these Bye-Laws; 


That any information or documents required by the Board under these Bye- 
Laws has or have not been duly furnished; 


That the Building Plan is not in accordance with these Bye-Laws framed by 
the Board under Section 260 or those framed under clauses (19) and (20) of 
section 348 of the Act; 


That the building would be an encroachment on defence land or land vested 
in the Cantonment Board;

(vi) That the site of the building does not abut on a street or projected street and

or path-way appertaining to such site; or 


(vii) That the erection or re-erection of the building is not in conformity with any

period of sanction.

and the Applicant that the construction will be carried out under his 
supervision according to the plans sanctioned by the Competent Authority.

The minimum size of the plot shall not be less than 50 sq.m. for residential 
purpose provided that for existing authorized buildings having a plot size of 
less than 50 sq.m., the said restriction shall not apply.

REVOCATION OF SANCTIONED BUILDING PLANS AND THE LIABILITY OF 
THE APPLICANTS TO CONFORM TO BYE-LAWS.—(1) The Competent 
Authority may revoke any building sanction issued under the provisions of these 
Bye-Laws, whenever there has been any false’ statement, or any 
misrepresentation of material fact, or concealment of facts in the application on 
which the building sanction was based, and the whole work executed on the basis 
of such sanction shall be treated as unauthorized. Further, any or all executed 
works in cases of deviation from the sanctioned plan shall be treated as 
unauthorized construction and would lead to the revocation of the accorded 
sanction. 


In the case of revocation of sanction during the course of construction under the 
above Bye-law, no compensation / damages shall be payable by the Competent 
Authority. 


No such order of revocation shall be passed by the Competent Authority without 
giving a reasonable opportunity to the applicant to be heard. 


NOTICE OF COMMENCEMENT AND COMPLETION OF WORK.—{1) On the 
issue of building sanction, the applicant shall commence the work for which the 
building sanction has been issued. The Applicant shall give notice to the Chief 
Executive Officer of his intention to start work on the building site by submitting 
Form F, set forth in the Appendix. 


On completion of work, the Applicant shall submit a Notice of Completion of the 
building as per Form G, set forth in the Appendix through the Architect / Engineer 
who has supervised the construction within a period of thirty days from the date of 
completion of work. 


COMPLETION-CUM OCCUPANCY CERTIFICATE.—{1)The Chief Executive 
Officer, on receipt of the notice of completion, shall inspect the work and issue a 
certificate of occupancy within the period stipulated in the Act, as per Form H, set 
forth in the Appendix, if the building has been constructed in conformity with the 
sanction issued. 


COMPOSITION OF UNAUTHORIZED CONSTRUCTION.—{1) The Cantonment 
Board may, by resolution, decide a formula to calculate the quantum of penalty / 
fees for the regularization of unauthorized construction. 


Such composition of unauthorized construction shall be subject to the provisions 
of the Cantonments Act, 2006, the rules subsisting there under and other 
restrictions mentioned in these Bye-Laws, including but not limited to FSI, 
Setbacks, and Ground Coverage. 


(art Wats 4] भारत का राजपत्र : असाधारण 


(3) In so far as constructions which are present and existing on the date of notifying of 


14. 


these Bye-Laws wherein such constructions are without sanction or in violation of 
approved plan including deviation, the applicants may apply to Cantonment Board 
for Compounding, which at the discretion of the Cantonment Board or GoC-in- 
Chief, as applicable, may be allowed subject to payment of compounding fees as 
determined by Cantonment Board or GoC-in-Chief, as applicable. This exercise 
shall be undertaken subject to these Bye-Laws framed hereunder after approval 
of the Central Government. 


CHAPTER IIl 
ZONING 
ZONING.—(1) The Anmedabad Cantonment is divided into two broad zones:- 


(a) Zone - |: This would include civil areas (Notified Civil Area) comprising 
civilian habitation. 


(b) Zone — II: All other land not included in Zone -1 but inclusive of all OGBs, 
Private land and leases situated outside Notified Civil Area. 


(2) Zone- I.— This Zone comprises all lands as depicted in Zonal Plan passed by the 


Board by resolution in this regard. 


(a) REQUIREMENT OF SITE.— No piece of land shall be used as a site for 
the construction of a building if the Competent Authority considers that the 
site is unsanitary, is not drained properly, or is incapable of being well 
drained. 


(0) PERMISSIBLE FLOOR SPACE INDEX (FSI).—The permissible Floor Space 
Index (FSI), depending on the plot size, shall be as per the table below:

shall be 2.75 m measured from the surface of the floor to the lowest point of the

shall not be more than 16 m from ground level including height of the stilt floor.

i) Parapet wall; 
ii) Roof water tanks and their supports; 
iii) Ventilation and air-conditioning structures; 
iv) Lift rooms and similar service equipment; 
v) Mumty /cabin of staircase; 
vi) Chimneys; 
vii) Architectural features not exceeding 1 metre in height.

CHAPTER IV 
SPECIFICITIES WITH RESPECT TO BUILDING CONSTRUCTION 


15. OPEN SPACES/SETBACKS.—The building envelope shall be governed by the 
following minimum clear open space and setbacks:- 


(1) FRONT SETBACK 


(a) 


Every building abutting a street/ footpath shall have a front setback from the 
front property line to the front building envelope or building front main wall. 


External staircases in the front elevation of the building shall not be 
considered in the front setback calculation. 


Sunshades and balconies shall be within the front property line or shall be 
constructed within the front setback or within the boundary wall of the plot. 


In the case of school buildings, the front boundary wall shall be recessed 
by 4.5 metre to accommodate visitor parking within the setback area. 


(2) SIDE AND REAR SETBACK 


(a) 


(b) 


(c) 


Every building shall have a minimum clear side and rear setback as 
prescribed in these Bye-Laws. No use is permitted in this open space 
except steps, staircases, rain water harvesting pits, septic tank and soak pit 
or in case this space is 3.0 m wherein parking of vehicles is allowed. 


External staircases in the sides and rear of the building shall not be 
considered in setback calculation and shall be as per the following table 
and shall be constructed within the boundary wall of the plot. 


Sunshades and balconies shall be within the boundary wall of the plot. 


The provisions for open spaces at the front, side and rear of the building shall be as 
per table below:

PARKING SPACE.—(1) Parking provision for different types of buildings shall be 
as per norms laid out by the National Building Code of India as amended from time 
to time, and it includes parking of all vehicles like cars, scooters, cycles and light 
and heavy commercial vehicles. 


These parking norms shall be applicable to all new applications for Building Plan 
sanctions. 


PLINTH OF MAIN BUILDING.—{1) The plinth of any part of a building shall be so 
located with respect to surrounding ground level that adequate drainage of the site 
is assured but not at a height of less than 0.45 m. 


PLINTH OF INTERIOR COURTYARDS.—Every interior courtyard shall be raised 
at least 0.15 m above the surrounding ground level and shall be satisfactorily 
drained. 


. SIZE OF HABITABLE ROOMS.—{1) No habitable room shall have an area of 


less than 9.50 sq.m. 
The width of the habitable Room shall be a minimum of 2.4 m 


In a two-room tenement one room shall not be less than 9.5 sq.m. and other 7.5 
sq.m. with minimum width of 2.1 m. 


. HEIGHT OF HABITABLE ROOM.—{1) The height of all rooms for human 


habitation shall not be less than 2.75 m measured from the surface of the floor to 
the lowest point of the ceiling i.e. bottom of the slab. 


In case of centrally air-conditioned building, height of the habitable room shall not 
be less than 2.40 m measured from the surface of the floor to the lowest point of 
air conditioning duct or false ceiling. 


[भाग 1--खण्ड 4] भारत का राजपत्र : असाधारण 111 


(3) In the case of pitched roof, the average height of rooms shall not be less than 2.75 
m The minimum clear headroom under a beam, folded plates or eaves shall be 
2.4m 


21. BATHROOMS AND WATER-CLOSETS (COMBINED).—{1) Size: The minimum 
size of a bathroom shall be 1.8 sq.m. and the minimum width of a side shall be 1.2 
m The minimum size of a water closet shall be 1.1 sq.m. and the minimum width 
of a side shall be 0.9 m The minimum size of a combined bathroom and water 
closet shall be 2.8 sq.m. and the minimum width of a side shall be 1.2 m 


S 


Height: The height of a bathroom or water closet measured from the surface of 
the floor to the lowest point in the ceiling i.e. the bottom of slab shall not be less 
than 2.1m 


@ 
— 


Other Requirements: A bathroom or water closet shall: 


(a) be so situated that at least one of its walls shall open to external air and shall 
have a minimum opening in the form of a ventilator or window or louvers not 
less than 0.3 sq.m. in area and side not less than 0.30 m; 


(0) not be directly under or above any room other than another water closet, 
bathroom, washing area or terrace unless it has a water tight floor; 


(c) have the platform and/or seat made of water tight non-absorbent material; 


(d) be enclosed by walls/partitions whose surface is finished with a smooth 
impervious material to a minimum height of 1.0 m above the floor level; 


(e) Be provided with an impervious floor finish, with floor surface sloping towards 
the drain with a suitable grade and not towards any other Habitable Room. 


(4) No room containing water closets shall be used for any purpose except as a 
lavatory and no such room shall open directly into a kitchen or cooking space by 
a door, window or other opening. Every room containing water closets shall have 
a door completely closing the entrance to it. 


22. KITCHEN.—{1) Size: The minimum area of the kitchen shall be 5.0 sq.m. and the 
minimum width of a side shall be 1.8 m The minimum area of the kitchen, which 
is also intended to be used as a dining room, shall be 7.5 sq.m. and the minimum 
width of a side shall be 2.1 mt 


(2) Height: The height of a kitchen measured from the surface of the floor to the lowest 
point in the ceiling i.e. bottom of slab shall not be less than 2.75 m 


(3) Other Requirements: Any room to be used as kitchen shall have: 
a) A height of not less than 2.75 m; 


b) A window the size of which shall not be less than one sixth of floor area opening 
directly to an interior or exterior open space, but not into a shaft. 


112 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART II—SEc.4] 


c) Unless separately provided for in a pantry, means for the washing up of kitchen 
utensils which shall lead directly or through a sink to a grated and trapped 
connection to the waste pipe. 


d) Be enclosed by walls/partitions whose surface is finished with a smooth 
impervious material to a minimum height of 1.5 metre above the floor level. 


e) Be provided with an impervious floor finish; with floor surface sloping towards 
the drain with a suitable grade and not towards any other habitable room. 


23. PANTRIES.—Pantries shall have:- 


(1) a floor area of not less than 3.0 sq.m. and a minimum width of a side shall be 1.4 
mt; 


(2) a sink for cleansing of kitchen's utensils which shall drain through a grated and 
trapped connection to the waste pipe; 


(3) been enclosed by walls/partitions whose surface is finished with a smooth 
impervious material to a minimum height of 1.0 m above the floor level; 


(4) been provided with an impervious floor finish, with floor surface sloping towards 
the drain with a suitable grade and not towards any other habitable room. 


24. LOFT.—(1) The maximum height of a loft shall be 1.5 m and the loft may be 
provided over corridors and passage ways only. 


(2) The clear head room under the loft shall not be less than 2.2 m; 


(3) Loft when provided in a commercial building, shall be located 2.0 m away from the 
entrance. 


25. MEZZANINE FLOOR.—(1) Size: Mezzanines shall not be sanctioned in 
residential buildings. When provided in commercial buildings, the aggregate area 
of the mezzanine floor shall not exceed 33.3 percent of the built-up area of that 
floor. 


(2) Height: The minimum height of a mezzanine floor shall be 2.2 m The head room 
under the mezzanine floor shall not be less than 2.2 m 


(3) Other Requirements: A mezzanine floor may be sanctioned over a room or a 
Compartment provided that: 


a) In mezzanine floor, rooms and any structure constructed with closed roof 
and side walls for any purpose shall be included in FSI but in case the 
mezzanine floor is constructed for interior architectural decorative purpose 
and other passage purposes with adequate handrails shall not be counted 
in FSI. 


[भाग खण्ड 4] 


भारत का राजपत्र : असाधारण 


113 


b) In case the size of mezzanine floor is 9.5 square metres or more, it should 
conform to the standard of living room in so far as lighting and ventilation 
is concerned. 


c) It is so constructed as not to interfere under any circumstances with the 
ventilation of the space over and under it; 


d) Such mezzanine floor or any part of it shall not be used as a Kitchen. 


e) Inno case shall a mezzanine floor be closed so as to make it liable to be 
converted into unventilated compartments. 


26. GARAGE.—(1) Individual private garage: The size of a private garage in 
residential building shall be not less than 2.5 m x 5.0 m. The garage if located in 
the side, open space shall not be constructed within 1.5 m from the main building. 


(2) Commercial / Public Garage: The size of a public garage shall be calculated 
based on the number of vehicles to be parked and the minimum parking space for 
each vehicle as specified below: 


Parking provision for different types of development shall be as per norms laid out 
in Table-7 below. The standards given are in Equivalent Car Space (ECS) as laid 
out by the National Building Code of India as amended from time to time and it 
includes parking of all vehicles like cars, scooter, cycles and light and heavy 
commercial vehicle.
